Per proposal then: a repeated producer is refused; `nate_validate_proposal_record` (:177-203) requires `iteration == handoff.round`, the bare role in `specialist` (echoed through `echo_bounded`, never interpolated whole), a non-empty summary, and provenance derived from seat evidence rather than the body's own claims — `evidence_ids` must be exactly the agent's `job_id`, `source_route_key` the `correlation_group`, `pid` absent, `parent_id`/`proposal_id` the Main and agent ids, `refined` false — else "proposal '{}' provenance fields do not match its native seat evidence"; under `omp-native-v1` any non-empty additive field is refused by `frozen_profile_field` naming the v2 profile. `nate_validate_proposal_artifact_matches_body` (:267) then re-parses the cached artifact bytes into `Proposal` and refuses inequality with the body — "proposal '{}' differs from its hashed native artifact".
/home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/kumitate-shokunin/corpus/knowledge/native-judge-pipeline.md:319:`validate_integrated_candidate` (:395) binds the candidate to the distiller: `candidate_digest` must equal `distiller.artifact_hash` and the cached bytes must re-parse byte-equal to `integrated_candidate` (:499-533) — deliberately no digest recompute, because the cache only holds bytes that already passed `validate_digest` and that equality is transitive. `nate_validate_integrated_survivor_set_and_digest` (:535-546) recomputes `hash_survivor_set` against `candidate_receipt.proposal_set_digest`, requires a non-empty duplicate-free survivor set in frozen roster order, and refuses a `duplicate_of` seat or a survivor whose advisory decision is not `Admit`. The effective vector then passes through `apply_subjective_argument_protocol`; an integrated malformed rejection returns early as an invalid handoff instead of an unreachable contribution arm; and `integrated_seat_admission_reason` (:468-497) maps the (advisory, selected, integrated) triple onto nine contribution reasons across ten arms — the tenth is the `unreachable!` guard — while `rejected_duplicate_fingerprint:<seat>` is minted by the caller before the match.

/home/vgpnk/Projects/shokunin/tsukuroi-shokunin/corpus/knowledge/adjacency-recheck-r66.md:19:3. **Quote located as the normalised substring** — the register's `quote` string is normalised (strip `**`, strip backticks, collapse whitespace runs, case-sensitive) and searched for in the normalised bytes of the live `PROVINCE.md`. The normalisation changes marker characters only; it does not fold case, punctuation, dashes or entities.
